# Text Elements
Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1 and the inequality stands
for all value of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1.
Hence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈  O𝑔(𝑛) is valid. 
Hence, the proof completes. ^RavkZBvc

Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1/2
and the inequality stands for all values of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1/2. 
Hence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈ Ω 𝑔(𝑛) is valid.
The proof completes ^B8GbsTRd

		{
			"id": "RavkZBvc",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -1033.3018301988247,
			"y": 579.7051222966119,
			"width": 475.2535400390625,
			"height": 100,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#e03131",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 0.5,
			"strokeStyle": "solid",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,
			"roundness": null,
			"seed": 254142155,
			"version": 251,
			"versionNonce": 1496429381,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1736596362990,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1 and the inequality stands\nfor all value of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1.\nHence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈  O𝑔(𝑛) is valid. \nHence, the proof completes.",
			"rawText": "Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1 and the inequality stands\nfor all value of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1.\nHence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈  O𝑔(𝑛) is valid. \nHence, the proof completes.",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 92,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1 and the inequality stands\nfor all value of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1.\nHence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈  O𝑔(𝑛) is valid. \nHence, the proof completes.",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},

		{

			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1736596362990,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1/2\nand the inequality stands for all values of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1/2. \nHence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈ Ω 𝑔(𝑛) is valid.\nThe proof completes",
			"rawText": "Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1/2\nand the inequality stands for all values of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1/2. \nHence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈ Ω 𝑔(𝑛) is valid.\nThe proof completes",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 92,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "Pick 𝑛0 = 1 and 𝑐 = 1/2\nand the inequality stands for all values of 𝑛 ≥ 𝑛0 and 𝑐 = 1/2. \nHence the statement 𝑓(𝑛) ∈ Ω 𝑔(𝑛) is valid.\nThe proof completes",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},
